### Quote PIT-Q115

DGFiP2026 annual administrative declaration overview; https://www.impots.gouv.fr/les-modalites-de-la-declaration-de-revenus-en-2026; nativeLF130–147; read 2026-10-29. literal excerpt
 
## Pourquoi dois-je continuer à déclarer malgré la mise en place du prélèvement à la source ?

Vous devez continuer à faire votre déclaration chaque année car l’administration fiscale ne dispose pas toujours de tous vos éléments actualisés comme les revenus fonciers ou les pensions alimentaires. Cette déclaration permet donc de régulariser votre situation de l’année précédente en prenant en compte tous vos revenus mais aussi vos charges (dons, dépenses éligibles aux crédits d’impôt...).
Dans la majorité des cas, si vos revenus n’ont pas changé ou si vous avez actualisé votre situation au fil de l’eau sur votre espace Finances publiques, cela ne change rien, vous n’aurez sans doute plus rien à payer. En revanche, si vous avez connu une augmentation de revenus mais ne l’avez pas signalée, vous aurez sûrement un reste à payer.
Dans certains cas, votre déclaration de revenus pourra aussi vous permettre de bénéficier d’un remboursement,si vous êtes éligible à un crédit d’impôt par exemple. Cette déclaration de revenus permettra également de mettre à jour votre taux de prélèvement à la source pour septembre, afin qu’il soit le plus adapté possible à votre situation.
## À quelle date le taux de prélèvement sera pris en compte ? Puis-je dès à présent le moduler ?

Le délai de prise en compte de votre nouveau taux de prélèvement à la source est de 1 à 2 mois.

Suite à votre déclaration de revenus, un nouveau taux sera calculé et sera appliqué dès le mois de septembre. Il prend en compte les éléments que vous avez déclarés. Ce taux vous est indiqué une fois votre déclaration en ligne signée ou mentionné sur votre déclaration automatique si vous ne corrigez rien.
Si vous estimez que ce taux n’est pas adapté à votre situation actuelle, vous pouvez l’adapter. Cliquez sur « Oui, je souhaite adapter mon prélèvement à la source » à la fin de votre déclaration de revenus.
## Si j'ai coché la case « Recevoir ma déclaration », quand vais-je recevoir ma déclaration papier ?

Si vous avez déclaré sur papier en 2025, votre déclaration papier vous sera envoyée courant avril 2026, si vous n'avez pas coché l'option « zéro papier » sur votre espace en ligne.

La déclaration papier n’est plus adressée aux usagers ayant déclaré de manière dématérialisée (Télé-IR ou Smartphone) leurs revenus en 2025.
Rappel : si votre résidence principale est équipée d’un accès à internet, votre déclaration de revenus doit être réalisée en ligne. Déclarer en ligne c'est profiter d'un délai supplémentaire et de la possibilité de corriger votre déclaration, mais aussi de tous les services en ligne qui sont associés à votre espace Finances publiques.
Toutefois, si vous estimez ne pas être en mesure de le faire, vous pouvez continuer à utiliser une déclaration papier.

### Quote PIT-Q116

DGFiP2026 annual administrative declaration overview; https://www.impots.gouv.fr/les-modalites-de-la-declaration-de-revenus-en-2026; nativeLF169–178; read 2026-10-29. literal excerpt
 
## Quelle échéance pour rectifier ma déclaration ?
### Si vous déclarez en ligne :

Pendant la période déclarative jusqu'à la date limite de votre département (voir calendrier de la déclaration en ligne) : vous pouvez effectuer autant de déclarations rectificatives que vous le souhaitez. Pour cela, retournez sur votre espace Finances publiques ou sur votre application mobile (pour les déclarations simples), puis cliquez sur "Accéder à la déclaration en ligne" puis "Corriger ".
Si vous êtes concerné par la déclaration automatique, vous pourrez corriger votre déclaration en ligne, dans l'éventualité où vous auriez oublié de modifier ou de compléter votre déclaration, pendant toute la période déclarative.
Une fois votre avis reçu ou disponible en ligne, si vous vous apercevez d’une erreur, vous pourrez de nouveau modifier ou corriger les éléments déclarés grâce au service de correction en ligne. Pour la déclaration des revenus 2025, le service sera disponible mi-août 2026 jusque mi-décembre 2026. Après cette date, vous ne pourrez plus modifier votre déclaration à partir du service de correction en ligne.
### Si vous déclarez papier :

Pendant la période déclarative : vous pouvez modifier votre déclaration de revenus, en adressant, jusqu'à la date limite de dépôt, une déclaration rectificative reprenant l'intégralité des rubriques vous concernant, auprès du service des impôts des particuliers dont vous dépendez.
Une fois votre avis reçu, vous pourrez effectuer une réclamation. Celle-ci pourra être qprésentée jusqu’au 31 décembre de la deuxième année suivant le dépôt de votre déclaration, soit en ligne depuis votre espace Finances publiques via la messagerie sécurisée, soit par courrier à votre centre des Finances publiques.

### Quote PIT-Q118

CSSL136-6/7/8 and CGI154quinquies; officialF2329 verified30June2026; https://www.service-public.gouv.fr/particuliers/vosdroits/F2329; nativeLF262–293; read 2026-10-29. literal excerpt
 
 À savoir 
 Si vous résidez en France, travaillez dans un pays de l' EEE : titleContent ( Union européenne : titleContent , Islande, Norvège, Liechtenstein) ou en Suisse et êtes affilié à la sécurité sociale obligatoire de ce pays, vous n'êtes pas soumis à la CSG et à la CRDS. Seul le prélèvement de solidarité de 7,5 % est dû.
 Comment payer les prélèvements sociaux sur les revenus du patrimoine et de placements ? 
 Le paiement varie selon qu'il s'agit de revenus du patrimoine (par exemple, revenus fonciers ) ou de revenus de placements (par exemple, revenus d'obligations).
 Revenus du patrimoine
 Si vous avez effectué votre déclaration de revenus dans les délais , vous recevez entre août et septembre un avis d'imposition commun pour vos prélèvements sociaux et votre impôt sur vos revenus.
 Cet avis unique présente les informations suivantes :
 Détail et calcul des 2 impositions
 Total du montant à payer
 Moyens de paiement que vous pouvez utiliser
 Date limite de paiement .
 Revenus de placements
 Vous n'avez pas de démarche à effectuer pour payer les contributions sociales sur ces revenus.
 Si les revenus proviennent de placements financiers, l'établissement payeur (banque ou organisme auprès de qui vous avez investi) prélève directement les contributions.
 Si les revenus proviennent de plus-values immobilières ou de certains biens meubles (métaux ou objets précieux par exemple), vous devez les déclarer aux services fiscaux qui se chargent du calcul des contributions.
 Dans quels cas une part de la CSG versée pour certains revenus du patrimoine est-elle déductible des revenus ? 
 Condition
 Pour bénéficier d'une CSG déductible, vous devez avoir choisi l'imposition au barème progressif pour vos revenus du patrimoine et de placements.
 Vous ne bénéficiez donc pas d'une CSG déductible si vous avez choisi l'option du Prélèvement forfaitaire unique (PFU), appelé également flat tax au taux de 31,4 % (ou de 30 % ).
 Part de CSG déductible
 Si vous avez payé des prélèvements sociaux sur certains revenus du patrimoine (revenus fonciers par exemple) et de placements, une part de la CSG versée au taux de 10,6 % (ou de 9,2 % ) est déductible de vos revenus.
 Cette part déductible est de 6,8 % .
 Les autres prélèvements sociaux ne sont pas déductibles de vos revenus :
 3,8 % (ou 2,4 % ) de part restante de CSG
 0,5 % de CRDS
 7,5 % de prélèvement de solidarité.
 Déduction
 La déduction se fait sur les revenus du patrimoine de l’année du paiement de la CSG.
 Ce montant est prérempli sur votre déclaration de revenus.
 Par exemple, vous pouvez déduire de vos revenus de 2025 (déclarés en 2026) la part déductible de la CSG payée en 2025 (calculée sur les revenus de 2024).
 À noter 
 Certains revenus de capitaux mobiliers soumis aux prélèvements sociaux lors de leur versement en 2025 peuvent aussi permettre de bénéficier d’une part de CSG déductible. Ce montant sera calculé automatiquement et ajouté au montant prérempli lors du calcul de l’impôt sur les revenus de 2025.

241-3 du code de la sécurité sociale, ainsi que des sommes versées aux plans d'épargne retraite qui sont exonérées en application du 18° de l'article 81.
Les membres d'un couple marié ou les partenaires liés par un pacte civil de solidarité défini à 【64†l'article 515-1 du code civil】, soumis à imposition commune, peuvent déduire les cotisations ou primes mentionnées au 1, dans une limite annuelle égale au total des montants déductibles pour chaque membre du couple ou chaque partenaire du pacte.
b) La différence, lorsqu'elle est positive, constatée au titre d'une année entre, d'une part, la limite définie au a et, d'autre part, les cotisations ou primes mentionnées au 1 peut être utilisée au cours de l'une des cinq années suivantes.

c) (Abrogé).
d) Les personnes qui, pour des raisons qui ne sont pas liées à la mise en oeuvre de procédures judiciaires, fiscales ou douanières, n'ont pas été fiscalement domiciliées en France au cours des trois années civiles précédant celle au cours de laquelle elles s'y domicilient bénéficient au titre de cette dernière année d'un plafond complémentaire de déduction égal au triple du montant de la différence définie au a.

II. – Les revenus d'activité professionnelle mentionnés au 1° du a du 2 du I s'entendent :
1. – Des traitements et salaires définis à 【65†l'article 79】 et des rémunérations allouées aux gérants et associés des sociétés mentionnées à 【66†l'article 62】, pour leur montant déterminé respectivement en application des articles 83 à 84 A et du dernier alinéa de l'article 62 ;
2. – Des bénéfices industriels et commerciaux définis aux 【67†articles 34 et 35】, des bénéfices agricoles mentionnés à l'article 63 et des bénéfices tirés de l'exercice d'une profession non commerciale mentionnés au 1 de l'article 92, pour leur montant imposable.
Les revenus exonérés en application des 【68†articles 44 sexies à 44 nonies, 44 terdecies à 44 septdecies】 ou au 9 de 【69†l'article 93】 ainsi que les abattements prévus à 【70†l'article 73 B】 sont retenus pour l'appréciation du montant des revenus définis au premier alinéa. Sont également retenus les revenus imposés dans les conditions prévues à 【71†l'article 151-0】 pour leur montant diminué, selon le cas, de l'abattement prévu au 1 de 【72†l'article 50-0】 ou au 1 de 【73†l'article 102 ter】.
Il n'est pas tenu compte des plus-values et moins-values professionnelles à long terme.
Conformément au II de l'article 10 de la loi n° 2026-103 du 19 février 2026 de finances pour 2026, la perte de recettes pour l'Etat résultant du I dudit article est compensée, à due concurrence, par la création d'une taxe additionnelle à l'accise sur les tabacs prévue au chapitre IV du titre Ier du livre III du code des impositions sur les biens et services.

### Quote PIT-Q122

Ministry PER guidance written1June2026; CGI163quatervicies/quinvicies; https://www.economie.gouv.fr/particuliers/gerer-mon-argent/gerer-mon-budget-et-mon-epargne/comment-fonctionne-le-plan-depargne-retraite-individuel; nativeLF95–104; read 2026-10-29. literal excerpt
 
### La fiscalité pour les sommes versées

Jusqu’à l’âge de 70 ans, les sommes versées sur un PER individuel au cours d'une année sont déductibles des 【20†revenus】 imposables de cette année, dans la limite d'un plafond global fixé pour chaque membre du foyer fiscal.

Le plafond personnalisé applicable est indiqué sur votre avis d'impôt à la rubrique « Plafond épargne retraite ». Il est indexé sur le plafond annuel de la Sécurité sociale (PASS) en vigueur.
Par ailleurs, à partir de 2026, l’utilisation des plafonds non mobilisés peut remonter jusqu’au cinq dernières années. Ainsi, vous pouvez mobiliser les plafonds de déduction non utilisés des cinq années précédentes à partir des sommes versées en 2026.

En revanche, la part non utilisée des plafonds des années 2024 et 2025 peut être utilisée uniquement dans un délai de 3 ans.

Enfin, les couples mariés ou pacsés peuvent mutualiser leurs plafonds respectifs.

Les a bis, a ter et b bis du 18° et le 18° bis de l'article 81, le deuxième alinéa du 2° de l'article 83, le dernier alinéa du I de l'article 154 bis, le deuxième alinéa du I de l'article 154 bis-0 A, l'article 163 bis AA et le d du 1 du I de l'article 163 quatervicies du présent code ne s'appliquent pas aux versements effectués par le titulaire du plan d'épargne retraite mentionné à l'article L. 224-1 du code monétaire et financier ou du sous-compte français du produit paneuropéen d'épargne-retraite individuelle mentionné à l'article L. 225-1 du même code à compter du jour de son soixante-dixième anniversaire.

Conformément au II de l'article 9 de la loi n° 2026-103 du 19 février 2026, ces dispositions, dans leur rédaction résultant du I du même article, s'appliquent à compter du 1^{er} janvier 2026.

### Quote PIT-Q124

CGI224; BOI-IR-CDHR-10; https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/15095-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-CDHR-10-20260630; nativeLF66–84; read 2026-10-29. literal excerpt
 
 B. Territorialité de la contribution
 1. Cas général
 20
 Sous réserve de l’application des conventions fiscales internationales destinées à éviter les doubles impositions, sont imposables à la CDHR les contribuables domiciliés fiscalement en France au sens de l’ article 4 B du CGI , passibles de l’impôt sur le revenu et qui disposent de revenus de source française ou étrangère entrant dans la composition du revenu de référence défini au II de l’ article 224 du CGI et supérieurs aux seuils d’imposition de la contribution.
 Pour plus de précisions sur les règles de domiciliation fiscale, il convient de se reporter au BOI-IR-CHAMP-10 .
 Il en résulte que les contribuables domiciliés fiscalement hors de France ne sont pas imposables à la contribution, quand bien même ils disposeraient de revenus de source française entrant dans la composition du revenu de référence.
 Remarque : La CDHR s’applique sous réserve des stipulations des conventions fiscales internationales, dès lors que le dispositif prévu à l’article 224 du CGI est dans le champ des conventions destinées à éviter les doubles impositions en matière d’impôt sur le revenu et sur la fortune.
 Les contribuables dits « non-résidents Schumacker », assimilés à des personnes fiscalement domiciliées en France au sens de la jurisprudence de la Cour de justice de l’Union européenne ( CJUE, arrêt du 14 février 1995, aff. C-279/93, « Finanzamt Köln-Altstadt c/ Roland Schumacker », ECLI:EU:C:1995:31 ), sont dans le champ d’application de la contribution. Toutefois, ils sont tenus à une obligation fiscale limitée, au sens des conventions internationales. Par suite, le revenu de référence servant d’assiette à la contribution est déterminé, pour ces contribuables, sur la base de leurs seuls revenus de source française.
 Pour plus de précisions, il convient de se reporter au BOI-IR-DOMIC-40 .
 30
 Pour l’application de la contribution, la France s’entend, du point de vue territorial :
 de la France continentale, des îles du littoral et de la Corse ;
 des départements et régions d’outre-mer (Guadeloupe et La Réunion) ;
 des collectivités territoriales uniques (Martinique, Guyane et Mayotte).
 En revanche, elle n’inclut pas les collectivités d’outre-mer de la République française, régies par l’ article 74 de la Constitution du 4 octobre 1958 (Polynésie française, Wallis-et-Futuna, Saint-Pierre-et-Miquelon et, sous réserve des dispositions particulières prévues par l’ article LO. 6214-4 du code général des collectivités territoriales (CGCT) et de l’ article LO. 6314-4 du CGCT qui prévoient une durée minimale de résidence de cinq ans, Saint-Barthélemy et Saint-Martin), les Terres australes et antarctiques françaises (TAAF) et l’île de Clipperton, régies par le dernier alinéa de l’ article 72-3 de la Constitution du 4 octobre 1958 , et la Nouvelle-Calédonie régie par l’ article 77 de la Constitution du 4 octobre 1958 .
 40
 La CDHR s’applique aux personnes de nationalité française résidant à Monaco, assujetties en France à l’impôt sur le revenu en application du 1 de l’ article 7 de la convention fiscale du 18 mai 1963 conclue entre la France et la principauté de Monaco (PDF - 244 Ko) ( I-B-1 § 220 du BOI-INT-CVB-MCO-10 ).
 50
 Pour les fonctionnaires des organisations internationales, la CDHR s’applique sous réserve des engagements internationaux souscrits par la France vis-à-vis de ces organisations.

parents) de l’un ou l’autre des époux.

Vos enfants mariés, liés par un Pacs ou chargés de famille peuvent
demander leur rattachement à votre foyer. lls ne peuvent pas être
rattachés à la fois à votre foyer et à celui des beaux-parents de
votre enfant. Faites-leur remplir une demande de rattachement et
conservez-la. Vous la produirez à la demande du service.

### Quote PASEXCLUDE

CGI204D/204H published BOFiP foreigncreditslimitedFrench/equalFrenchtaxincome excludedPAS; https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/11247-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-PAS-20-20-10-20260407; nativeLF103–105; read 2026-10-31. 
 
Le numérateur est, le cas échéant, diminué du montant des crédits d’impôt prévus par une convention fiscale internationale, égal à l’impôt acquitté à l’étranger et correspondant à des revenus inclus dans le champ d’application du prélèvement à la source.
Toutefois, le montant de ces crédits d’impôt n’est retenu que dans la limite de l’impôt français correspondant aux mêmes revenus et effectivement imputables (CIétranger) (pour plus de précisions, il convient de se reporter à l’exemple d’application exposé au 【32†I-C-3 § 130】).
En revanche, les crédits d’impôt prévus par une convention fiscale internationale et dont le montant est égal à celui de l’impôt français correspondant à un revenu de source étrangère perçu ou réalisé par un résident de France ne sont pas concernés. Les revenus qui y ouvrent droit sont exclus du champ d’application du prélèvement à la source (【33†BOI-IR-PAS-10-20】).

### Quote SALARYMIN

CGI83 published DGFiP salary10% lower-remuneration exception; https://www.impots.gouv.fr/particulier/questions/comment-puis-je-beneficier-de-la-deduction-forfaitaire-de-10; WebL[24, 39]; read 2026-10-31. 
 
L24: La déduction forfaitaire de 10% simplifie la prise en compte des frais professionnels en couvrant les dépenses les plus courantes et est appliquée automatiquement à tous les salariés et à certains dirigeants de sociétés.
L25: ## Une déduction automatique
L26: 
L27: Cette déduction forfaitaire de 10% est automatiquement retranchée des salaires déclarés par chacun des membres de votre foyer fiscal. Vous n'avez donc rien à faire.
L28: 
L29: Elle couvre les dépenses professionnelles courantes liées à l'emploi ou à la fonction, comme :
L30:   * les frais de déplacements du domicile au lieu de travail ;
L31:   * les frais de restauration sur le lieu de travail (dépenses supplémentaires par rapport au coût des repas pris au domicile) ;
L32:   * l’achat de documentation personnelle (non fournie par votre employeur).
L33: Chaque membre de votre foyer peut renoncer individuellement à cette déduction forfaitaire, et demander, sous certaines conditions, la déduction de ses frais réels. La déduction de 10% ne peut jamais être appliquée en même temps que la déduction des frais réels.
L34: ### Limites de la déduction
L35: 
L36: Pour chaque salarié, le montant de la déduction de 10% s'élève :
L37: 
L38:   * au minimum, à 509 € (sauf si la rémunération déclarée est inférieure) ;
L39:   * au maximum, à 14 555 €.

### Quote CGI1724

CGI1724 final whole-euro liquidation; fraction0.50up; https://www.legifrance.gouv.fr/codes/article_lc/LEGIARTI000006312863; nativeLF29–38; read 2026-10-31. 
 
Article 1724

EN VIGUEUR DEPUIS LE 31 MARS 2002

Modifié par Règlement CE 974-98 1998-05-03 art. 14 JOCE 11 mai 1998 en vigueur le 1er janvier 2002


La liquidation de toutes sommes à recevoir, à quelque titre et pour quelque cause que ce soit, est arrondie à l'euro le plus proche. La fraction d'euro égale à 0,50 est comptée pour 1.

Il est procédé à cet arrondissement au niveau du décompte de chaque impôt ou taxe.

| current_main62b7639a | 111 | **The computation, in order.** Never skip a step. | {"current_native_LF": [112], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 113 | 1. Put each income in its category and take the category allowance: salaries and unemployment benefit (lines 1AP to 1DP) take the 10% deduction or actual expenses; pensions take the 10% pension allowance, capped per household; micro-entrepreneurs and furnished lets take the flat allowance of their micro regime; dividends taxed at the scale take the 40% allowance. | {"current_native_LF": [114], "named_current_destinations": ["method1"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L15 correct wage/unemployment boxes"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 114 | 2. Add the net category incomes to get the revenu brut global. | {"current_native_LF": [115], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 115 | 3. Take off the deductions from global income (PER contributions, maintenance paid, deductible CSG, earlier global deficits) to get the revenu net imposable (RNI). The PER ceiling is per person, can be carried forward for 3 years, and a couple can pool ceilings by ticking box 6QR. | {"current_native_LF": [96, 116], "named_current_destinations": ["PER", "method3"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L21 temporal PER carry qualification, no blanket2026three-year carry"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 116 | 4. Count the shares (CGI art. 194 and 195): 1 for a single person, 2 for a couple taxed jointly; plus a half share for each of the first two dependent children and a full share from the third. A child in alternating residence gives a quarter share for each of the first two and a half share from the third. Case T (living alone with the children) gives a full share for the first child. Case L (living alone, no dependant, having raised a child alone for at least 5 years) gives a half share. Invalidity and veteran status give extra half shares. | {"current_native_LF": [117], "named_current_destinations": ["shares"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L17 ordinary widow count restored"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 117 | 5. Divide the RNI by the shares, apply the scale to one share, multiply back. Income taxed under the quotient system for exceptional income is added here. | {"current_native_LF": [118], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 118 | 6. Cap the family quotient: work out the tax again on the base shares only (1 or 2), less the cap for each extra half share. Keep the higher of the two results. | {"current_native_LF": [119], "named_current_destinations": ["cap"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L17 correct1sharecapbenchmark/2011boundedcomplement/deathyear2shareexception"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 119 | 7. Apply the overseas abatement if the household lives in an overseas department on 31 December. | {"current_native_LF": [120], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 120 | 8. Apply the décote if the tax is below the threshold. It is the fixed amount less 45.25% of the tax, and cannot exceed the tax. | {"current_native_LF": [121], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 121 | 9. Take off tax reductions (they stop at zero), then tax credits (any excess is refunded). Check the overall cap on tax advantages: gifts and advantages tied to the personal situation are outside it; the home help and childcare credits, SME, FCPI, FIP, Pinel and Denormandie reductions are inside it. Pinel covers homes bought or built from 1 September 2014 to 31 December 2024, so 2025 brings only the continuing instalments of earlier investments. | {"current_native_LF": [122], "named_current_destinations": ["method9"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L19 aid/subceiling/householdcaporder"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 122 | 10. Add the flat tax (12.8%) on investment income not taxed at the scale, then the CEHR, then the CDHR. | {"current_native_LF": [123], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 123 | 11. Take off the withholding and instalments already paid in the income year, and the January advance of credits, to get the balance or the refund. | {"current_native_LF": [124], "named_current_destinations": ["method11"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L01 January advance ADDback"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 125 | **Investment income.** Dividends, interest and gains on securities are taxed by default at the flat 12.8% income tax rate (CGI art. 200 A, 1). The household can instead opt, on the return and by the filing deadline, to put all of them under the scale (CGI art. 200 A, 2). The option is global: it covers every item in the flat-tax field for that year. It brings the 40% dividend allowance and the 6.8% deductible CSG. It helps a household with no tax or a marginal rate of 11%; at 30% or more the flat rate is lower (F2613). Social levies are charged on top in both cases and are not IR. | {"current_native_LF": [134], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 127 | **Exceptional and deferred income (CGI art. 163-0 A).** On request (line 0XX of form 2042 C), one quarter of a non-recurring income is added to ordinary income and the extra tax on that quarter is multiplied by four. The income must exceed the average net taxable income of the three previous years, except for items with no amount condition such as the taxable part of severance pay. Arrears of salary, pensions or rent use a coefficient of the number of years concerned plus one. A household already at 45% on its ordinary income gains nothing. | {"current_native_LF": [136], "status": "Exact old unit still present; no substantive loss"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 129 | **CEHR.** Charged on the revenu fiscal de référence (RFR), counting quotient-system income at its full amount. If the RFR is at least 1.5 times the average of the two previous years, and each of those years was at or below EUR 250,000 (single) or EUR 500,000 (couple), the excess over the average is halved, the contribution worked out, and that extra contribution doubled (CGI art. 223 sexies, II). The CEHR applies until the year in which the public deficit is nil (loi n° 2011-1977, art. 2, III-A). | {"current_native_LF": [138], "named_current_destinations": ["cehr"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L03 complete3cumulativegates and familyreconstruction"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 131 | **CDHR (contribution différentielle sur les hauts revenus).** Created by loi n° 2025-127 (art. 10) for the income of 2025 and amended by loi n° 2026-103 (art. 2) for the income of 2026 onward. It applies to households domiciled in France whose CDHR reference income passes EUR 250,000 (single) or EUR 500,000 (couple). That reference income is the RFR, without the quotient rules, less certain allowances and exempt items (CGI art. 224, II). The contribution is the positive difference between: | {"current_native_LF": [140], "status": "Exact old unit still present; no substantive loss"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 133 | - 20% of the reference income, less a décote where that income is at or below EUR 330,000 (single) or EUR 660,000 (couple). The décote is 20% of the income less 82.5% of (income less EUR 250,000 or EUR 500,000), if positive; and | {"current_native_LF": [142], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 134 | - the IR, plus the CEHR, plus certain final withholding taxes, plus EUR 1,500 per dependant and EUR 12,500 for a couple. | {"current_native_LF": [143], "status": "Exact old unit still present; no substantive loss"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 136 | From the income of 2026, the year of moving abroad or into France has its own rules (CGI art. 224, V bis). For the income of 2025, a liable household paid an advance of 95% of its estimate between 1 and 15 December 2025. Refer any household near the CDHR threshold: the reference income needs a full working paper. | {"current_native_LF": [145], "status": "Exact old unit still present; no substantive loss"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 138 | **Social levies.** These are not IR, but they ride on the same income. Never quote one rate without naming the income and the year: investment income of 2025 bears 17.2%; investment income of 2026 bears 18.6% in the general case; rental income, gains on real property, PEL and CEL interest and life-insurance products stay at 17.2%. A resident who works in another EEA state or Switzerland and is covered by its social security pays only the 7.5% solidarity levy (F2329). Computation is in `fr-capital-gains` and `fr-rental-income`. | {"current_native_LF": [147], "named_current_destinations": ["social"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L04 category/collection/year source correction"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 140 | **Self-employed (pointer).** A micro-entrepreneur declares turnover on form 2042-C-PRO and the tax office applies the allowance in the rates table. The micro regime is lost only if a limit was exceeded in both of the two previous years (F23267). The flat payment option (versement forfaitaire libératoire) is paid with the Urssaf return and replaces IR on that turnover. It is open where the household RFR of year N-2 is below the limit in the rates table, and is taken or dropped by telling Urssaf before 30 September of the year before. Real-regime results are in `fr-income-tax`. | {"current_native_LF": [92, 149], "named_current_destinations": ["micro", "VFL"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L02 actual applicable RFRyear"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 142 | **Salary terminology trap.** The amount on line 1AJ is the salaire net imposable from the payslip, not the gross and not the net paid. The 10% deduction then comes off line 1AJ. | {"current_native_LF": [151], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |

| current_main62b7639a | 158 | Forms: 2042 (main return), 2042 C (extra income and options), 2042 C PRO (self-employed), 2042 RICI (reductions and credits), 2047 (foreign income), 2044 (real-regime rental income). The return is compulsory every year whatever the income; a household with no change may be on the automatic return. Online filing is the rule where the home has internet access. | {"current_native_LF": [169], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 160 | **Withholding at source (prélèvement à la source).** The tax of the year is collected during the year: | {"current_native_LF": [171], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 162 | - **Retenue à la source** on salaries, pensions, unemployment benefit and sick pay, by the payer. | {"current_native_LF": [173], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 163 | - **Acomptes** (monthly, or quarterly on option) on self-employed profits, rental income, maintenance received, annuities and foreign income, debited by the DGFiP and based on the income declared the year before. | {"current_native_LF": [174], "status": "Exact old unit still present; no substantive loss"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 164 | - **The rate** is the household rate from the last return (CGI art. 204 H): the rate of year N-2 from January to August, of year N-1 from September. A couple taxed jointly gets individualised rates automatically, for each partner's own income; common income such as rent stays at the household rate. Either partner can opt for the household rate. | {"current_native_LF": [175], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 165 | - **Default grid.** With no personal rate, the payer applies the grid in CGI art. 204 H, III (separate grids for Guadeloupe, La Réunion and Martinique, and for Guyane and Mayotte). A taxpayer may also choose the grid for salaries to keep their rate private, and then pays any shortfall themselves by the end of the following month. | {"current_native_LF": [176], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 166 | - **Zero rate.** Where IR was nil for the last two known years and the RFR of the last known year is below EUR 29,310 per share (CGI art. 204 H, II). | {"current_native_LF": [177], "named_current_destinations": ["paszero"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L11 indexedperiodsplit"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 167 | - **Changes.** Report a marriage, Pacs, birth or adoption, death, divorce or end of a Pacs within 60 days. Raising the rate is free. Lowering it is allowed only if the estimated levy for the year is more than 5% lower than the levy without the change (CGI art. 204 J, III-1). A change applies at the latest from the third month after the request. | {"current_native_LF": [178], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 168 | - **Settlement.** The spring return computes the final tax; withholding is set against it, and the balance is collected or refunded as in the table above. Keep bank details up to date before 1 July. | {"current_native_LF": [179], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |

| current_main62b7639a | 185 | For IR, late interest starts on 1 July of the year after the income year (CGI art. 1727, IV-1). It is not due where the shortfall is no more than one twentieth of the base, save deliberate breach (art. 1727, II-4), and it is halved for a spontaneous corrective return paid in full (art. 1727, V). Interest is not a penalty: it runs on top of any surcharge. In 2026 the administration may reassess the income of 2023, 2024 and 2025 (aide-mémoire); longer periods apply in some cases (see `fr-tax-audit`). | {"current_native_LF": [198], "status": "Exact old unit still present; no substantive loss"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 189 | - **Non-residents.** Taxed on French-source income only, with a floor of 20% on taxable income up to the top of the 11% band and 30% above (14.4% and 20% on income received in the overseas departments). A non-resident whose average rate on worldwide income is lower may ask, on line 8TM, to be taxed at that average rate. The brochure prints the band top for the income of 2025 as EUR 29,574, five euros below the EUR 29,579 in CGI art. 197; the law wins, so use EUR 29,579 and flag the difference. Shares, décote and reductions for a non-resident, and treaty relief, are outside this skill: see `fr-cross-border`. | {"current_native_LF": [202], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 190 | - **Online deadline.** Non-residents file online by the first date (21 May 2026); residents abroad filing on paper by 19 May 2026. | {"current_native_LF": [203], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 191 | - **CDHR.** Applies only to households domiciled in France, subject to treaties; "Schumacker" non-residents treated as residents fall within it on French-source income only (BOFiP BOI-IR-CDHR-10). From the income of 2026 it reaches the year of departure and the year of arrival (CGI art. 224, V bis). | {"current_native_LF": [204], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 192 | - **Foreign income.** Declared on form 2047 and paid by acompte during the year. Foreign tax on dividends and interest is credited against the 12.8% flat tax within the treaty credit (CGI art. 200 A, 1-A-1°). | {"current_native_LF": [205], "status": "Exact old unit still present; no substantive loss"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 193 | - **Social levies for EEA or Swiss social security members.** Only the 7.5% solidarity levy, not CSG and CRDS. | {"current_native_LF": [206], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 194 | - **Leaving France.** The exit tax on shareholdings is in `leaving-france-exit-tax`. | {"current_native_LF": [207], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 198 | **Example 1: a single employee, simple case, income of 2025.** A single person (1 share) has EUR 42,000 on line 1AJ and no other income. The 10% deduction is EUR 4,200, inside the EUR 509 floor and the EUR 14,555 ceiling, so the net taxable income is EUR 37,800. The scale gives EUR 1,977.69 on the 11% band (EUR 17,979 at 11%) and EUR 2,466.30 on the 30% band (EUR 8,221 at 30%): EUR 4,443.99. That is above the EUR 1,982 single-person threshold, so there is no décote. EUR 4,000 was withheld in 2025, so EUR 443.99 is left to pay. That is above EUR 300, so it is taken in four debits from September to December 2026. | {"current_native_LF": [213], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 200 | ```oa-check<br>name: France 2026 return, single employee, income of 2025, salary EUR 42,000<br>inputs:<br>  salary: 42000<br>  withheld: 4000<br>steps:<br>  - deduction: min(max(salary * 0.10, 509), 14555)<br>  - rni: salary - deduction<br>  - band_11: (min(rni, 29579) - 11600) * 0.11<br>  - band_30: max(min(rni, 84577) - 29579, 0) * 0.30<br>  - gross_tax: band_11 + band_30<br>  - decote: min(max(897 - gross_tax * 0.4525, 0), gross_tax)<br>  - tax: gross_tax - decote<br>  - balance: tax - withheld<br>expected:<br>  deduction: 4200.00<br>  rni: 37800.00<br>  gross_tax: 4443.99<br>  decote: 0.00<br>  tax: 4443.99<br>  balance: 443.99<br>``` | {"current_native_LF": [215], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 223 | **Example 2: a couple with two children, the family-quotient cap bites, plus dividends under the flat tax.** A married couple with two dependent children (3 shares) has net taxable income of EUR 90,000 and EUR 10,000 of dividends received in 2025, left under the flat tax. With 3 shares, each share is EUR 30,000, taxed at EUR 2,103.99, so EUR 6,311.97 in all. With the 2 base shares, each share is EUR 45,000, taxed at EUR 6,603.99, so EUR 13,207.98, less two extra half shares at EUR 1,807: EUR 9,593.98. That is higher than EUR 6,311.97, so the cap applies and the tax from the scale is EUR 9,593.98. It is far above the EUR 3,277 couple threshold, so no décote. The dividends bear 12.8% income tax (EUR 1,280) and 17.2% social levies (EUR 1,720), 30% in all. The IR for the year is EUR 10,873.98. Had the same dividends been received in 2026, the levies would be 18.6% (EUR 1,860), 31.4% in all. | {"current_native_LF": [238], "named_current_destinations": ["case2"], "status": "Exact named source-backed root correction/qualification; not byte-exact preservation", "reason": "FR-L04 original fixture now explicitly source-collected2025; all3oa exact"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 225 | ```oa-check<br>name: France 2026 return, couple with 2 children, family-quotient cap, dividends under the flat tax<br>inputs:<br>  rni: 90000<br>  dividends: 10000<br>steps:<br>  - per_share_3: rni / 3<br>  - tax_3_shares: ((min(per_share_3, 29579) - 11600) * 0.11 + max(min(per_share_3, 84577) - 29579, 0) * 0.30) * 3<br>  - per_share_2: rni / 2<br>  - tax_2_shares: ((min(per_share_2, 29579) - 11600) * 0.11 + max(min(per_share_2, 84577) - 29579, 0) * 0.30) * 2<br>  - capped: tax_2_shares - 2 * 1807<br>  - gross_tax: max(tax_3_shares, capped)<br>  - decote: min(max(1483 - gross_tax * 0.4525, 0), gross_tax)<br>  - flat_tax_ir: dividends * 0.128<br>  - total_ir: gross_tax - decote + flat_tax_ir<br>  - levies_2025: dividends * 0.172<br>  - levies_if_2026: dividends * 0.186<br>expected:<br>  tax_3_shares: 6311.97<br>  tax_2_shares: 13207.98<br>  gross_tax: 9593.98<br>  decote: 0.00<br>  flat_tax_ir: 1280.00<br>  total_ir: 10873.98<br>  levies_2025: 1720.00<br>  levies_if_2026: 1860.00<br>``` | {"current_native_LF": [240], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 253 | **Example 3: the décote, single person, income of 2025.** A single person (1 share) has net taxable income of EUR 20,000. The scale gives (20,000 - 11,600) x 11% = EUR 924. That is below the EUR 1,982 single-person threshold, so the décote is EUR 897 less 45.25% of EUR 924 (EUR 418.11): EUR 478.89. The tax after the décote is EUR 445.11 (CGI art. 197, I-4-a; BOFiP BOI-IR-LIQ-20-20-30-20260407). | {"current_native_LF": [268], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| current_main62b7639a | 255 | ```oa-check<br>name: France 2026 return, single person, décote, income of 2025, RNI EUR 20,000<br>inputs:<br>  rni: 20000<br>steps:<br>  - gross_tax: (min(rni, 29579) - 11600) * 0.11<br>  - decote: min(max(897 - gross_tax * 0.4525, 0), gross_tax)<br>  - tax: gross_tax - decote<br>expected:<br>  gross_tax: 924.00<br>  decote: 478.89<br>  tax: 445.11<br>``` | {"current_native_LF": [270], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 16 | # France: personal income tax for a household (impôt sur le revenu) | {"current_native_LF": [29], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 18 | This skill computes French personal income tax (impôt sur le revenu, IR) for a household resident in France: the scale, the family quotient and its caps, the décote, the deductions, reductions and credits, the two high-income contributions (CEHR and CDHR), and withholding at source. Figures are for tax year 2026. France taxes the previous year's income, so the scale, the family-quotient caps, the décote, the allowances and the deduction limits below are those that govern the income of 2025, declared in spring 2026; the scale for the income of 2026 has not been published yet, and where a figure that governs income of 2026 is already printed (the micro turnover limits, the retirement-savings ceiling, the social levies on investment income), its row says so. Withholding at source and the social levies are stated as in force on 19 October 2026. Self-employed income in depth is in `fr-income-tax`; investment income and gains on securities in `fr-capital-gains`; rental income in `fr-rental-income`; crypto-assets in `fr-crypto-tax`; leaving France and the exit tax in `leaving-france-exit-tax`; an audit or a reassessment in `fr-tax-audit`. | {"current_native_LF": [31, 35, 38, 169], "named_current_destinations": ["scope", "years", "forms", "companions"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Tax/compliance population, year and form routing retained; currency/authority labels are context, not new tax rules"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 45 | ## The method, step by step | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 47 | 1. **Fix the household and the year.** Work on one foyer fiscal and one income year. For the return filed in 2026, that is the income of 2025, on form 2042 and its annexes, filed online by the date for the household's département in the deadline table above (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/les-modalites-de-la-declaration-de-revenus-en-2026). Marital status is taken at 31 December and dependants at 1 January or, if they increased during the year, at 31 December (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/05-situation_foyer_75a84.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 48 | 2. **Put each income in its category and apply the category allowance.** Salaries take the 10% deduction or actual expenses; unemployment benefit (lines 1AP to 1DP) is taxed under the salary rules and takes the same deduction; pensions take their own allowance, capped per household; micro-entrepreneurs and furnished lets take the flat allowance of their regime. The allowances are in the tables in Section 4 (https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/10855-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-BAREME-000035-20260217 and https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/aide_memoire.pdf and https://entreprendre.service-public.gouv.fr/vosdroits/F23267). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 49 | 3. **Add the net category incomes and take off the deductions from global income** (retirement savings, maintenance payments, deductible CSG, earlier global deficits) to reach net taxable income, using the tables in Section 10 (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/12-charges_ded_rev_225a236.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 50 | 4. **Count the shares (parts) of family quotient** under CGI art. 194 and 195, using Section 5 (https://www.service-public.gouv.fr/particuliers/vosdroits/F2705). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 51 | 5. **Apply the scale to one share and multiply by the number of shares**, using the scale table in Section 3 (https://www.service-public.gouv.fr/particuliers/vosdroits/F1419). Income taxed under the quotient system for exceptional income is added at this step (Section 11, https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/17-divers_321a336.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 52 | 6. **Cap the family-quotient advantage.** Work out the tax again with the base shares only (1 or 2), subtract the cap for each extra half share, and keep the higher of the two results (https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/2494-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-LIQ-20-20-20-20260407). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 53 | 7. **Apply the décote** if the gross tax after the cap is below the décote threshold for the household (https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/2495-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-LIQ-20-20-30-20260407). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 54 | 8. **Take off the tax reductions** (they cannot go below zero), then the tax credits (any excess is refunded), and check the overall cap on tax advantages (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/plafonnement.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 55 | 9. **Add the flat-rate taxes** on income taxed outside the scale (for example the income tax part of the flat tax on investment income, https://www.service-public.gouv.fr/particuliers/vosdroits/F2613), **then the CEHR and the CDHR** where the reference income passes their thresholds (https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/7804-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-CHR-20170711 and https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/15095-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-CDHR-10-20260630). | {"current_native_LF": [114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 121, 122, 123], "named_current_destinations": ["method1", "method2", "method3", "shares", "method5", "cap", "decote", "method9", "method10"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same ordered household computation, expanded DOM/source rules"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 56 | 10. **Reconcile with withholding at source.** Subtract what was withheld or paid in instalments during the income year, and the January advance of credits, to find the balance due or the refund (https://www.service-public.gouv.fr/particuliers/vosdroits/F34009). | {"current_native_LF": [124], "named_current_destinations": ["method11"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "FR-L01 source-backed sign correction: January advance added back, not deducted twice"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 58 | ## Ask the client first | {"current_native_LF": [312], "status": "Exact actual old wording retained in successor"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 60 | - Were you resident in France for tax purposes for the whole of 2025? | 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 61 | - What was your situation on 31 December 2025, and did you marry, enter a Pacs, divorce, separate or lose a spouse during 2025? | 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 62 | - Who lived with you and depended on you on 1 January 2025 and on 31 December 2025, and is any child in alternating residence? | 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 160 | The 10% deduction is worked out automatically for each member of the household on the total of the sums that person declares in the category of salaries (traitements et salaires); actual expenses can be claimed instead where they are higher (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/06-traitements_salaires_85a114.pdf). Unemployment benefit, including the ARE, belongs to that category: it is declared on lines 1AP to 1DP, not on lines 1AJ to 1DJ, and the deduction is computed on it with the salaries (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/06-traitements_salaires_85a114.pdf). Pensions do not take this deduction; they take their own allowance below. | {"current_native_LF": [83, 114, 151], "named_current_destinations": ["salary", "method1", "salarytrap"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Salary deduction/own-person computation and salary-versus-unemployment form distinction (FR-L15)"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 275 | The advantage that each extra share gives is capped. Work out (a) the tax with all the household's shares and (b) the tax with the base shares only (1 for a single person, 2 for a couple), less the cap for each extra half share. The caps are in the two tables below; the service-public page rounds the quarter-share cap. | {"current_native_LF": [60, 61, 62, 63, 64, 119], "named_current_destinations": ["cap", "caphalf", "capT", "capL", "capinvalid", "capwidow"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Same cap rules, quarter-share exact-half method and bounded special complements"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 310 | ## Section 6: Décote (relief for low tax bills) | {"current_native_LF": [65, 121], "named_current_destinations": ["decote", "decoterates"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Thresholds/order/45.25%formula retained"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 312 | The décote applies to every household, whatever its situation, when its gross tax is below the threshold for its situation in the table below. It is worked out on the tax given by the scale, including tax on income taxed under the quotient system, after the family-quotient cap and before the tax reductions (https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/2495-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-LIQ-20-20-30-20260407). The décote equals the fixed amount for the household less 45.25% of the gross tax, and it is deducted from the gross tax. | {"current_native_LF": [65, 121], "named_current_destinations": ["decote", "decoterates"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Thresholds/order/45.25%formula retained"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 333 | ## Section 7: CEHR (contribution exceptionnelle sur les hauts revenus) | {"current_native_LF": [69, 138], "named_current_destinations": ["cehr", "cehrrate"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "CEHR rates/reference-income/quotient-fullamount; FR-L03 restores complete cumulative smoothing gates"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 335 | The CEHR is charged on the household's revenu fiscal de référence (reference income), not on net taxable income, and is added to the income tax (https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/7804-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-CHR-20170711). It is charged by bracket: the lower rate on the part of reference income inside the first bracket, the higher rate on the part above it. For the income of 2025, income taxed under the quotient system counts at its amount before division by the quotient, and a smoothing calculation applies where the reference income of 2025 is at least one and a half times the average of the reference income of 2023 and 2024 and other conditions are all met (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/17-divers_321a336.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [69, 138], "named_current_destinations": ["cehr", "cehrrate"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "CEHR rates/reference-income/quotient-fullamount; FR-L03 restores complete cumulative smoothing gates"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 337 | **CEHR: contribution exceptionnelle sur les hauts revenus (CGI art. 223 sexies)** | {"current_native_LF": [69, 138], "named_current_destinations": ["cehr", "cehrrate"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "CEHR rates/reference-income/quotient-fullamount; FR-L03 restores complete cumulative smoothing gates"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 349 | ## Section 8: CDHR (contribution différentielle sur les hauts revenus) | {"current_native_LF": [71, 140], "named_current_destinations": ["cdhr", "cdhrrate"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "CDHRpositive difference and relevant reference/family/décote inputs"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 351 | The CDHR ensures that households with a high reference income pay a minimum average rate of income tax. It was created by the 2025 finance law (loi n° 2025-127, art. 10) for the income of 2025 and is charged again on the income of 2026 under the 2026 finance law (loi n° 2026-103, art. 2), as the BOFiP page in force from 30 June 2026 records (https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/15095-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-CDHR-10-20260630). It applies to taxpayers domiciled in France for tax purposes whose reference income for the CDHR passes the threshold for their situation. | {"current_native_LF": [71, 140], "named_current_destinations": ["cdhr", "cdhrrate"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "CDHRpositive difference and relevant reference/family/décote inputs"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 353 | The contribution is the positive difference between (1) the minimum rate applied to the CDHR reference income, less a décote where that income is at or below the décote limit, and (2) the income tax, the CEHR and certain final withholding taxes, plus a fixed amount per dependant and a fixed amount for a couple. For a single person the décote is the minimum rate applied to the reference income less the décote factor applied to the excess of the reference income over the single-person liability threshold, if the result is positive; for a couple the excess is measured over the couple threshold (https://bofip.impots.gouv.fr/bofip/15095-PGP.html/identifiant=BOI-IR-CDHR-10-20260630). The CDHR reference income is not the ordinary revenu fiscal de référence: it is that figure reduced by certain allowances and adjusted as the BOFiP page sets out (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/17-divers_321a336.pdf). All amounts are in the tables below. | {"current_native_LF": [71, 140], "named_current_destinations": ["cdhr", "cdhrrate"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "CDHRpositive difference and relevant reference/family/décote inputs"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 355 | **CDHR: contribution différentielle sur les hauts revenus (CGI art. 224)** | {"current_native_LF": [71, 140], "named_current_destinations": ["cdhr", "cdhrrate"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "CDHRpositive difference and relevant reference/family/décote inputs"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 376 | For the income of 2025, a household liable to the CDHR had to pay an advance between 1 and 15 December 2025, based on income received to 1 December 2025 plus an estimate for the rest of December, and the advance is set against the contribution due (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/actualite/contribution-differentielle-sur-les-hauts-revenus-cdhr). This skill does not state the advance rule for the income of 2026: check the administration's page in the autumn. | {"current_native_LF": [75, 140], "named_current_destinations": ["cdhradvance", "cdhr"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "2025advance95%December1–15 supplied;2026advance remains expressly unconfirmed, no invented zero"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 507 | ### The overall cap on tax advantages (plafonnement global) | {"current_native_LF": [104, 122], "named_current_destinations": ["globalcap", "method9"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "10k+8k cap and included/excluded benefits"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 509 | The total of the advantages that fall under the cap is compared with the cap; any excess is added to the tax due. The advantages linked to the taxpayer's personal situation, and those for an aim of general interest without anything in return (gifts, patronage), are outside the cap. The credits for employing someone at home and for childcare outside the home are inside the cap, as are the reductions for investment in SMEs, FCPI and FIP, Pinel and Denormandie (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/plafonnement.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [104, 122], "named_current_destinations": ["globalcap", "method9"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "10k+8k cap and included/excluded benefits"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 520 | ## Section 11: Special cases | {"current_native_LF": [136], "named_current_destinations": ["quotient"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Exceptional/deferred quotient request/coefficient/gates/topband effect"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 522 | ### Exceptional income: the quotient system (CGI art. 163-0 A) | {"current_native_LF": [136], "named_current_destinations": ["quotient"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Exceptional/deferred quotient request/coefficient/gates/topband effect"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 524 | Income that by its nature is not received every year can, on the taxpayer's request on line 0XX of form 2042 C, be taxed under the quotient system: one quarter of the net exceptional income is added to the ordinary net global income, and the extra tax due on that quarter is multiplied by four. The coefficient is always four. The income must be higher than the average of the net taxable income of the three previous years (for income received in 2025, the average of 2022, 2023 and 2024), except for income with no amount condition, among them the taxable part of severance pay on voluntary departure, retirement, early retirement or dismissal. Income taxed at a proportional rate is excluded (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/17-divers_321a336.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [136], "named_current_destinations": ["quotient"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Exceptional/deferred quotient request/coefficient/gates/topband effect"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 526 | Deferred income (arrears of salary, pensions or rent received in one year for earlier years) can also be taxed under the quotient system whatever its amount; its coefficient is the number of years of normal payment plus one (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/17-divers_321a336.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [136], "named_current_destinations": ["quotient"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Exceptional/deferred quotient request/coefficient/gates/topband effect"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 528 | The quotient system gives nothing to a household already taxed at the top rate of the scale on its ordinary income alone, because the extra quarter falls in the same band. | {"current_native_LF": [136], "named_current_destinations": ["quotient"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Exceptional/deferred quotient request/coefficient/gates/topband effect"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 530 | ### Non-residents | {"current_native_LF": [202], "named_current_destinations": ["nonresident"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Own197A floor/average-rate pointer and source printed five-euro discrepancy"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 532 | A non-resident is taxed only on French-source income. The tax from the scale cannot be lower than the minimum rates in the table below; a non-resident whose average rate on worldwide income is lower may ask, on line 8TM, to be taxed at that average rate instead (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/04-declaration_revenus_64a73.pdf). The minimum rate applies up to the top of the second band of the scale table in Section 3. The brochure page prints that limit for the income of 2025 as a figure that differs by five euros from the scale page; this skill relies on the band itself, not on the brochure figure. The household position of a non-resident (shares, décote, reductions) and treaty relief are outside this skill: refer. | {"current_native_LF": [202], "named_current_destinations": ["nonresident"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Own197A floor/average-rate pointer and source printed five-euro discrepancy"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 546 | The tax from the scale, after the family-quotient cap and before the décote and the reductions, is reduced by an abatement fixed by the address on 31 December of the income year (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/21-calcul_impot_369a382.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [68, 120], "named_current_destinations": ["DOM", "method7"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "DOM address31December/order30/40%andcaps"} |

| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 558 | ### Year of marriage or Pacs | {"current_native_LF": [44], "named_current_destinations": ["marriage"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Marriage/Pacs full-year optional separate, earlierPacs exception"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 560 | A couple who marry or enter a Pacs during the year file one joint return for the whole year, with all the income each of them had over the whole year. They may instead opt, irrevocably and only for that year, for two separate returns, each partner then being treated as single for the whole year. A couple in a Pacs before 2025 who marry in 2025 stay taxed jointly and cannot opt (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/05-situation_foyer_75a84.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [44], "named_current_destinations": ["marriage"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Marriage/Pacs full-year optional separate, earlierPacs exception"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 562 | ### Year of divorce, separation or end of a Pacs | {"current_native_LF": [45], "named_current_destinations": ["divorce"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Separate returns/T/L31December"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 564 | The former partners file two separate returns for the year of the divorce, separation or end of the Pacs. For the case T or case L half share, the condition of living alone is judged on 31 December, so each former partner may claim it if the other conditions are met (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/05-situation_foyer_75a84.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [45], "named_current_destinations": ["divorce"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Separate returns/T/L31December"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 566 | ### Year a spouse or partner dies | {"current_native_LF": [46, 117, 119], "named_current_destinations": ["death", "shares", "cap"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Deathperiod returns and explicit FR-L17 death-year share/cap distinction"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 568 | The couple are taxed jointly from 1 January to the date of death, and the survivor is taxed separately for the rest of the year (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/05-situation_foyer_75a84.pdf). | {"current_native_LF": [46, 117, 119], "named_current_destinations": ["death", "shares", "cap"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Deathperiod returns and explicit FR-L17 death-year share/cap distinction"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 570 | ### Time limit for reassessment (droit de reprise) | {"current_native_LF": [198], "named_current_destinations": ["audit"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Ordinary2026reassessment2023–25, longer-event companion"} |
| 5bb7b11889a978b3652f56a0558bbcac5f71fc2c^ | 572 | In 2026 the administration may reassess the income of 2023, 2024 and 2025 (https://www.impots.gouv.fr/www2/fichiers/documentation/brochure/ir_2026/pdf_som/aide_memoire.pdf). Longer periods apply in some cases, for example undeclared activity: see `fr-tax-audit`. | {"current_native_LF": [198], "named_current_destinations": ["audit"], "status": "Manually routed actual old topic; archive/qualification noted", "reason": "Ordinary2026reassessment2023–25, longer-event companion"} |
